````text
=================================================================
 the_entire_enchilada_v1-5.txt
 UPDATES SINCE: the_entire_enchilada_v1-4.txt
 SCOPE:         NET DELTA ONLY (what actually changed vs V1.4)
=================================================================

This file chronicles ONLY the changes made to the live project since
`the_entire_enchilada_v1-4.txt` was produced. It is not a full dump of
the code. Each section below lists what changed for one file, the exact
deltas, and any new code that was added. Files that did NOT change are
listed at the end so it is clear nothing was missed.

=================================================================
 1. THE ONE BIG CHANGE (summarized)
=================================================================
Two user-driven features landed this round:

  A. THE TESTS CAN NOW RUN INSIDE CHAT.HTML. In V1.4 the saved chat
     tests could only be run as a VERDICT LIST on the index page. The
     user asked for a way to INJECT a saved test into chat.html so the
     actual assistant output can be seen in the real chat UI. Resolution:
     - The Configuration page's per-agent card got a "Run tests in chat
       ->" button that opens
           chat.html?agent=<id>&runTests=1
     - chat.html gained a saved-test RUN MODE (a new JS section). It
       plays every ENABLED test for that agent as real chat messages,
       driven through the existing sendTurn() pipeline (so bubbles,
       session tracking, transcripts and save all work), with a pinned
       status banner + Stop button and a verdict summary at the end.
     - Additionally, the appearance of chat.html now mirrors the index
       page's typography settings (see feature B below).

  B. AN APPEARANCE SETTINGS SECTION. A new "Appearance" section on the
     Configuration page with ONE global font family + base font size,
     applied to BOTH the index page and chat.html. Both pages read the
     stored appearance settings on load and apply them through CSS
     variables, and the primary text sizes were converted to rem so the
     size knob visibly scales the whole UI. The section has a live
     preview and a Save button that persists via the merging
     POST /api/settings.

=================================================================
 2. FILE: static/js/ui/chat-tests.js           (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
One net addition: a second button on each agent's runner block,
"Run tests in chat ->" (chosen over running a single test; runs ALL
enabled tests for the agent):

    const chatRunBtn = document.createElement("button");
    chatRunBtn.type = "button";
    chatRunBtn.className = "btn btn-secondary btn-small";
    chatRunBtn.textContent = "Run tests in chat \u2192";
    chatRunBtn.title =
        "Opens chat.html in run mode and plays every enabled test for this agent " +
        "as real chat messages so you can see the output.";
    chatRunBtn.style.marginLeft = "6px";
    runnerWrap.appendChild(chatRunBtn);

    chatRunBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const url = "chat.html" +
            "?agent=" + encodeURIComponent(agent.id) +
            "&runTests=1";
        window.open(url, "_blank");
    });

The existing inline verdict runner, Enable/Disable All, step builder,
test persistence and per-agent cards are unchanged.

=================================================================
 3. FILE: static/chat.html                    (HEAVILY CHANGED)
=================================================================
This round added feature A's run mode and feature B's typography.

3.1  NEW "18b. SAVED-TEST RUN MODE" JS section
     (between application boot and section 19 event wiring).

  State + Stop:
     let testRunActive = false;
     let testRunCancelled = false;

     function stopTestRun() {
         testRunCancelled = true;
         const btn = DOM.messages.querySelector(".sc-test-run-stop");
         if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
         setSaveStatus("Stopping after the current step...", "");
     }

  Boot wiring (inside boot()): when ?runTests=1, the auto-hi prefill and
  the ?prefill= handling are skipped and the runner starts instead:

     if (params.get("runTests") === "1") {
         startTestRunInChat();
     } else {
         maybeAutoHi();
     }

     const prefillText = params.get("prefill");
     if (params.get("runTests") !== "1" && prefillText && prefillText.trim()) { ... }

  Test selection - same rules as the index runner:
     const mine = allTests.filter(test =>
         (test.enabled !== false) &&
         (!test.agentId || test.agentId === activeAgent.id)
     );

  Re-added (they were deleted with the old test engine in V1.4):
     testScript(test)     - steps[] or the legacy input-string splitter
     checkExpectation()   - string (substring, case-insensitive), regex
                            (new RegExp(v, "i")), type: nonEmpty | number

  The play loop drives the REAL send pipeline (sendTurn, the same one
  the composer uses) so every step is a real visible bubble, one server
  session per run (the first step creates it, later steps reuse it):

     finalReply = await sendTurn(script[index]);
     setWaiting(false);

  A pinned banner is appended into the thread:
     DOM.messages.appendChild(banner);  // .sc-test-run-banner w/ Stop btn
     setBanner("Running tests for " + activeAgent.name + "...", 0);

  Per-step expectations abort that test with the offending step number;
  a per-test try/catch marks it Error and the run continues; Stop breaks
  after the current step. After the loop the banner is removed and a
  summary system bubble is appended:

     Saved-test run for <agent>: N/M passed.
     [PASS]  test name
     [FAILED]  test name - Expected "x" in reply. (step 2)
     ...

  setSaveStatus() shows the summary (ok = all passed). Composer input is
  protected from mid-run typing: handleSend() early-returns while
  testRunActive.

3.2  NEW "17b. APPEARANCE" JS section (before boot):

     function applyAppearance(appearance) { ... }

  It maps the stored appearance key onto this page's root CSS vars
  (setting them, or REMOVING them so the :root defaults kick in):

     root.style.setProperty("--app-font-family", font);   // when non-empty
     root.style.setProperty("--app-font-size",  size+"px");// when > 0
     (removeProperty otherwise)

  Called in boot() right after the /api/settings load:
     applyAppearance(settings);

3.3  CSS DENS / typography scaling:

       (Badges, timestamps and drop-down chrome stay fixed px.)
     - NEW .sc-test-run-banner / .sc-test-run-stop rules (flex status

renderAppearance() builds:

        const appearanceMount = document.createElement("div");
````

````text
=================================================================
 the_entire_enchilada_v1-6.txt
 UPDATES SINCE: the_entire_enchilada_v1-5.txt
 SCOPE:         NET DELTA ONLY (what actually changed vs V1.5)
=================================================================

This file chronicles ONLY the changes made to the live project since
`the_entire_enchilada_v1-5.txt` was produced. It is not a full dump of
the code. Each section below lists what changed for one file, the exact
deltas, and any new code that was added. Files that did NOT change are
listed at the end so it is clear nothing was missed.

=================================================================
 1. THE ONE BIG CHANGE (summarized)
=================================================================
A full visual "face lift" of BOTH user-facing pages plus a real dark
theme, per the user's choices (light + dark toggle, full component
refresh, green/teal accent over the blue base):

  - THEME SYSTEM. Appearance now stores light / dark / system ("system"
    follows the OS via prefers-color-scheme). Both pages set
    data-theme on <html>; every component reads the palette through
    CSS variables, and each page has a [data-theme="dark"] override
    block, so the entire UI re-skins without any markup-level changes.
  - APPEARANCE SECTION grows a "Theme" select (Light / Dark / System),
    with live preview on change; saved to the existing merging
    POST /api/settings. localStorage caches the theme for pre-paint
    application to avoid a light-flash for dark users.
  - PALETTE. New token set: blue base (#0b6bcb) with a branded
    green/teal accent (#0d9488 / #2dd4bf in dark) used for highlights,
    active states, chat agent avatars and gradients. Larger radii
    (small 6 / medium 10 / large 16 / pill) and soft layered shadows.
  - COMPONENTS. Index gets a gradient hero header, card monogram
    avatars with hover lift + teal accent edge, gradient buttons,
    teal focus rings, pill status dots, section-heading accent tiles,
    themed scrollbars. chat.html gets a gradient top bar, welcome
    hint chips, teal/soft user bubbles, accent agent bubbles with
    gradient avatars, a pill gradient composer + gradient Send button,
    and an accent-tinted right panel.

=================================================================
 2. FILE: static/js/ui/appearance.js           (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
  - NEW exports / behaviour:
      applyTheme(theme) sets document.documentElement.dataset.theme to
      "light"|"dark" (resolving "system" through a
      "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" matchMedia) and installs a one-time
      change listener that keeps following the OS while the stored theme
      is "system" (module-level currentTheme).
      applyAppearance(appearance) now also applies the theme:
          const theme = (appearance && appearance.theme) || "system";
          applyTheme(theme);
  - NEW THEME_OPTIONS     = system / light / dark.
  - renderAppearance():
      - A new "Theme" <select id="appearance-theme"> is rendered ABOVE
        the existing font controls; change fires applyTheme(value) as a
        live preview (no save).
      - The Save payload now includes theme: themeSelect.value.
      - After a successful save, localStorage.setItem("appearance-theme",
        theme) is written (wrapped in try/catch for private mode).
      - The intro copy now mentions "theme + typography".
  - values() now also returns theme.
  - The rest (font/size select + preview + status styling) is unchanged.

=================================================================
 3. FILE: static/config/app_settings.json      (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
The appearance key now carries a theme field (seeded to "system"):

    "appearance": {
        "theme": "system",
        "fontFamily": "",
        "fontSize": 0
    },

The saved chat test (custom-mtnwyh6t-krgy, scoped to dev_assistant,
3 steps) is untouched.

=================================================================
 4. FILE: static/index.html                    (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
Only one addition: a pre-paint theme script in <head> before the
stylesheet link:

    <script>
        (function () {
            var s = "system";
            try { s = localStorage.getItem("appearance-theme") || "system"; } catch (e) {}
            var dark = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;
            document.documentElement.dataset.theme =
                s === "dark" || s === "light" ? s : (dark ? "dark" : "light");
        })();
    </script>

It flips data-theme before CSS applies so dark users don't see a light
flash; app.js re-applies the authoritative server settings on boot.

=================================================================
 5. FILE: static/js/ui/agents.js               (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
Agent cards now open with a monogram avatar chip:
  - NEW helper monogram(name): one word -> first 2 letters; else the
    first letter of the first and last words; uppercase; "?" if empty.
  - buildCard() inserts, before the <h3>:
        const avatar = document.createElement("div");
        avatar.className = "card-avatar";
        avatar.textContent = monogram(agent.name || "?");
  Everything else in the card (badge, description, Chat button ->
  window.open("/static/chat.html?agent=...")) is unchanged.

=================================================================
 6. FILE: static/css/styles.css                (HEAVILY CHANGED)
=================================================================
6.1  :root token overhaul + DARK THEME block.
     - Old tokens replaced with the new palette (blue base, teal accent):
       --color-primary #0b6bcb, --color-primary-dark #0a5cb3,
       NEW --color-primary-soft, --color-accent #0d9488,

       --color-pre-bg/-text, --color-input-bg, --color-avatar/-text,
       --color-online, --color-header-grad-a/b.
     - Radii up: small 6px, medium 10px, large 16px, NEW pill 999px.
     - NEW layered shadows: --shadow-soft/-panel/-card/-pop/-modal.
     - NEW [data-theme="dark"] block overriding the same token names:
       bg #0d1117, surface #151c25, alt #1a222d, hover #202a37,
       borders #242e3b/#33404f, text #e6edf3/#c3cdd8/#8b98a9/#5b6b7d,
       primary #3b82f6 (brighter for dark), accent #2dd4bf, accent-soft
       rgba(45,212,191,.14), danger #f87171, user bubble #0f2f2b/#1d4942,
       input bg #0f151e, header gradient #1d4ed8 -> #0f766e.
     - NEW themed scrollbars (both themes) on ::-webkit-scrollbar*.

6.2  Component refreshes (all token-driven):

 7. FILE: static/chat.html                     (HEAVILY CHANGED)
=================================================================
````

````text
=================================================================
 the_entire_enchilada_v1-8.txt
 UPDATES SINCE: the_entire_enchilada_v1-7.txt
 SCOPE:         NET DELTA ONLY (what actually changed vs V1.7)
=================================================================

This file chronicles ONLY the changes made to the live project since
`the_entire_enchilada_v1-7.txt` was produced. It is not a full dump of
the code. Each section below lists what changed for one file, the exact
deltas, and any new code that was added. Files that did NOT change are
listed at the end so it is clear nothing was missed.

=================================================================
 1. THE BIG CHANGE (summarized)
=================================================================
Two coupled rounds of work, both requested by the user through the app:

  - FUNCTIONAL RAG MEMORY. The prototype `rag/` module (ingest/search/
    main) is now wired into the live server as a persistent memory store
    the agents can recall with the `search_chat_logs` tool:

      * A brand-new config module, `app/paths.py`, is the single source
        of truth for every runtime folder. The UI configuration
        (`static/config/app_settings.json`) can now relocate the data
        folder, the chat-save transcripts folder, and the RAG database
        (`dataDir`, `chatSavePath`, `ragDbPath`). Absolute values are
        used verbatim; relative values resolve against the project root.
        This also fixed a real bug: the first `resolve_path()` version
        joined an absolute configured path onto the fallback, so
        `chatSavePath: "E:\\saved from app"` was silently dropped.

      * A brand-new `app/rag_commit.py` commits ONE finalized chat
        transcript into the store, and exposes purge/rebuild/status so
        the store can be forgotten or re-indexed without hand-hacking
        the chroma folder.

      * Chat sessions now carry a `rag` flag ("save to memory"). A
        per-chat toggle in the chat header controls it; the default
        comes from the new `rag.commitOnSave` setting (default false).
        On finalize, chats marked for memory are committed into the
        RAG store (never blocks the save itself).

      * `search_chat_logs` self-heals an EMPTY store exactly once per
        process by indexing every transcript - but only when the new
        `rag.autoIngest` setting is on (default true).

      * New server endpoints `GET /api/rag/status`,
        `POST /api/rag/rebuild`, `POST /api/rag/reset`, and new
        `scripts/rebuild_rag.py` CLI (`build|purge|status`).

      * A new discovered agent `agent_library/rag_assistant/` - a
        dedicated memory-recall chat agent that uses `search_chat_logs`.

  - FRONTEND CONFIG + STORE MANAGEMENT. The configuration panel gained
    Data folder / RAG database path fields, two RAG behavior toggles,
    and a live RAG-store status line with "Forget everything" /
    "Rebuild memory" buttons. The flyout chat got a configurable
    header toggle ("Save to memory") and the JS API client grew
    `ragStatus()` / `rebuildRag()` / `resetRag()`.

  - DOCS RE-SYNC. `README.md`, `documentation/CREATING_AGENTS.md`,
    `documentation/_make_snapshot.py` and `documentation/APP_SNAPSHOT.md`
    were updated to capture the new RAG architecture, and this delta
    file (v1-8) was written.

=================================================================
 2. NEW FILE: app/paths.py                              (ADDED)
=================================================================
Config-driven path resolution module. Adds the keys to
`static/config/app_settings.json`:

        dataDir        base data folder (default "data"); relative ->
                       project root, absolute -> used as-is
        chatSavePath   where saved chat transcripts (.txt) are written;
                       empty -> <dataDir>/chatlog/agent-text-records
        ragDbPath      where the RAG store (chroma.sqlite3) lives;
                       empty -> <dataDir>/rag_db
        rag            { commitOnSave: bool, autoIngest: bool }

Module-level resolved constants: `BASE_DIR`, `DATA_DIR`, `CHATS_DIR`,
`CHAT_SAVE_PATH`, `RECORDS_DIR`, `CHAT_RECORDS_DIR` (alias), `RAG_DB_DIR`,
`LOG_FILE`, `ACTIVE_SESSION_FILE`, `HISTORY_FILE`, `EXPORTS_DIR`,
`APP_SETTINGS_FILE`, `SERVER_SETTINGS_FILE`. Live functions:

    resolve_path(raw, *fallback_parts) -> Path   # empty raw -> BASE_DIR/parts
    _rooted(raw, default: Path) -> Path          # empty raw -> default
    rag_config(default_commit=None) -> dict      # commitOnSave + autoIngest
    about() -> dict                              # human-readable resolved paths

The `_rooted` helper is the corrected core: a present configured value is
used verbatim (`Path(text)` when absolute, `BASE_DIR / text` when
relative); nothing is re-joined onto it. `resolve_path` keeps the fallback
semantics for `dataDir`. Paths resolve at import time - restart the server
after editing path settings (rag behavior toggles read live).

=================================================================
 3. NEW FILE: app/rag_commit.py                        (ADDED)
=================================================================
Commit / maintain the persistent RAG store. Public API:

    commit_transcript(transcript: Path) -> str   # ingest ONE file, upsert
    purge_store() -> str                          # delete store contents
    rebuild_store() -> str                        # re-index all transcripts
    status() -> dict                              # path + chunk count + config
    _chunk_count() -> int                         # direct sqlite count

`commit_transcript` is called from `store._finalize_locked`; it ingests the
just-written transcript file (`rag.ingest.ingest_file`), upserts the
segments through `rag.search.RAGStorage(persist_dir=paths.RAG_DB_DIR)` and
is wrapped in try/except by the caller so a failed commit never blocks the
chat save. `purge_store` removes `chroma.sqlite3` /
`fallback_vector_db.json` plus leftover segment dirs. `status()` also
returns `paths.rag_config()` and `paths.about()` for the UI.

=================================================================
 4. FILE: app/chat_store/store.py                 (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
  4.1  All path constants now come from `app.paths`:
          DATA_DIR        = paths.DATA_DIR
          CHATS_DIR       = paths.CHATS_DIR
          RECORDS_DIR     = paths.RECORDS_DIR
          LOG_FILE        = paths.LOG_FILE
          ACTIVE_SESSION_FILE = paths.ACTIVE_SESSION_FILE
          APP_SETTINGS_FILE   = paths.APP_SETTINGS_FILE
       (module docstring row "_LOG_FILE / ACTIVE_SESSION_FILE / RECORDS_DIR
        <- app/paths" added).

  4.2  `rag` flag through the session lifecycle:
       - `ensure_session(agent, session_id, title, new_chat, rag=None)` -
         new `rag: bool | None` parameter. `None` -> the stored
         `commitOnSave` default applies (the comment explains this).
       - `_create_locked(agent, title="", rag=None)` resolves
             if rag is None:
                 rag = paths.rag_config()["commitOnSave"]
         then stamps the new session dict with
             "rag": bool(rag),
         and the startup log line becomes
             print(f"[CHATS] started session '{session['id']}' for "
                   f"'{session['agentId']}' (rag={session['rag']})")
       - `finalize_session(title=None, rag=None)` and
         `_finalize_locked(title=None, rag=None)` accept the override and,
         after writing the transcript + metadata log:
             # Commit to the RAG store when the chat is marked for memory ...
             commit = rag if rag is not None else bool(session.get("rag", False))
             if commit:
                 try:
                     from app.rag_commit import commit_transcript
                     commit_transcript(target)
                 except Exception as e:
                     print(f"[CHATS] RAG commit failed (chat still saved): {e}")
       The save itself is unaffected - the commit is best-effort.

=================================================================
 5. FILE: app/chat_store/logger.py                 (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
`DEFAULT_LOG_FILE` (and the session log file) are now resolved through
`app.paths` so the UI-configured data folder controls the JSONL location,
with a static fallback when `app.paths` cannot be imported:

    try:
        from app import paths as _app_paths
    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
        _app_paths = None
    DEFAULT_LOG_FILE = (
        _app_paths.LOG_FILE
        if _app_paths is not None
        else Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "data" / "chatlog" / "chatRecord.jsonl"
    )

=================================================================
 6. FILE: app/tools/search.py                     (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
The hardcoded `ROOT_DIR / data / ...` paths are replaced with `app.paths`;
the self-heal is now gated behind `rag.autoIngest`.

      6.1  New constants / helpers at the top:
               from app import paths
               RAG_DB_DIR = paths.RAG_DB_DIR
               CHAT_RECORDS_DIR = paths.CHAT_RECORDS_DIR
               _rag_ingested = False

               def _auto_ingest_enabled() -> bool:
                   """Only explicit commits / rebuild when the UI turned
                   auto-loading off."""
                   return bool(paths.rag_config()["autoIngest"])

      6.2  `_ensure_rag_ingested(storage)` now returns early when
           auto-ingest is disabled:
               if _rag_ingested:
                   return True
               if not _auto_ingest_enabled():
                   return False

      6.3  `search_chat_logs` builds the storage from
           `RAGStorage(persist_dir=str(RAG_DB_DIR))` (was
           `ROOT_DIR / "data" / "rag_db"`). Everything else unchanged.

=================================================================
 7. FILE: server.py                              (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
  7.1  Imports + constants now resolve through app/paths:
           from app import paths
           DATA_DIR = paths.DATA_DIR
           HISTORY_FILE = paths.HISTORY_FILE
           EXPORTS_DIR = paths.EXPORTS_DIR
           APP_SETTINGS_FILE = paths.APP_SETTINGS_FILE
       (comment: "Folder locations resolve through app.paths so the UI
        configuration can relocate the data folder.")

  7.2  ChatRequest gains the per-chat memory flag:
           rag: bool = False         # commit this chat to the RAG memory
                                     # store on save

  7.3  The /api/chat handler passes it through:
           session = chat_store.ensure_session(
               agent, session_id=data.session_id, title=data.title,
               new_chat=data.new_chat, rag=data.rag)

  7.4  /api/chats/end accepts a `rag` override (bool only; None falls back
       to the chat's stored flag / commitOnSave default):
           rag = payload.get("rag")
           row = chat_store.finalize_session(
               title=payload.get("title") or payload.get("chatTitle"),
               rag=rag if isinstance(rag, bool) else None)

  7.5  NEW "RAG MEMORY STORE" block (three endpoints, importing from
       app.rag_commit lazily inside each handler):
           @app.get("/api/rag/status")     -> {"status": status()}
           @app.post("/api/rag/rebuild")   -> {"message": rebuild_store()}
           @app.post("/api/rag/reset")     -> {"message": purge_store()}

=================================================================
 8. FILE: rag/ingest.py                        (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
New `ingest_file(file_path, chunk_size=500, overlap=50)` extracted so ONE
transcript can be indexed (per-chat commits) as well as a whole folder.
`ingest_directory` now loops `ingest_file` per `.txt`:

    for txt_file in txt_files:
        all_chunks.extend(ingest_file(txt_file, chunk_size=chunk_size, overlap=overlap))

`ingest_file` reads the file, parses header + turns, wraps each sliding
chunk with the `[title | Turn N | speaker]` context label, and returns the
`{text, raw_chunk, metadata}` dicts. No behavioral change to chunking.

=================================================================
 9. FILE: rag/search.py                        (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
In `RAGStorage.add_chunks`, the per-document insert switches `add` -> upsert
(with `add` as fallback) so re-committing a chat version or re-running a
rebuild overwrites segments instead of raising duplicate-id errors.

=================================================================
 10. FILE: rag/main.py                         (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
The CLI now resolves its defaults through `app.paths` when running inside
the Terminator1 app, keeping cwd-relative fallbacks when standalone:

    try:
        from app import paths as app_paths
        default_chat_logs = str(app_paths.CHAT_RECORDS_DIR)
        default_persist = str(app_paths.RAG_DB_DIR)
    except Exception:
        default_chat_logs = "./data/chatlog/agent-text-records"
        default_persist = "./data/rag_db"

The prompt uses `default_chat_logs`; persistence uses `default_persist`.

=================================================================
 11. NEW FILE: scripts/rebuild_rag.py                 (ADDED)
=================================================================
No-arg CLI for store maintenance; the actual work is reused from
`app.rag_commit`:

    python scripts/rebuild_rag.py [build|purge|status]
      build   (default) re-index every transcript into the store
      purge   delete the store so it starts empty (transcripts kept)
      status  show store path + chunk count + current RAG behavior

Bootstraps `sys.path` with the project root, imports `app.paths` + the
commit functions, and prints the per-command summary.

=================================================================
 12. NEW FILES: agent_library/rag_assistant/          (ADDED)
=================================================================
A new auto-discovered agent for chat-memory recall.

  agent.json:
      {
        "id": "rag_assistant",
        "name": "RAG Assistant",
        "mode": "agent",
        "model": "gemma4:e2b",
        "description": "Chat-memory assistant that recalls past sessions by
                        searching the local RAG database.",
        "tools": ["search_chat_logs", "get_current_date"]
      }

  agent.md:
      A "RAG Assistant" personality document. It instructs the agent to use
      `search_chat_logs` to recall past sessions, translate temporal
      keywords ("yesterday", "last session", "a few days ago") into
      topical queries, and fix typos before searching. Includes an
      example Q/A flow and "just retrieve - do not fabricate" guidance.

 13. FILE: static/js/classes/chat-window.js   (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
The flyout widget hides the side panel (`rightPanel` is forced off), so
per-chat controls had nowhere to live. Added a config-driven header toggle:

  13.1  Config surface: `config.headerToggle` ({ name, label, value }).
        In `_buildHeader`, after the flyout agent-select (because the
        widget's panel is disabled in flyout mode) it renders:
            <label class="cw-header-toggle">
              <input type="checkbox" data-name="{name}" checked={value}>
              <span class="cw-header-toggle-label">{label}</span>
            </label>
        and stores the checkbox element on `this._headerToggle`.

  13.2  `getPanelValues()` now merges the header toggle in, so the app
        reads `widget.getPanelValues().ragCommit` uniformly whether the
        panel exists or not:
            getPanelValues() {
                const values = this.panel ? this.panel.getValues() : {};
                if (this._headerToggle) {
                    values[this._headerToggle.dataset.name] =
                        this._headerToggle.checked;
                }
                return values;
            }

  13.3  `this._headerToggle = null` added to the DOM reference cache.
````

````text
=================================================================
 the_entire_enchilada_v1-9.txt
 UPDATES SINCE: the_entire_enchilada_v1-8.txt
 SCOPE:         NET DELTA ONLY (what actually changed vs V1.8)
=================================================================

This file chronicles ONLY the changes made to the live project since
`the_entire_enchilada_v1-8.txt` was produced. It is not a full dump of
the code. Each section below lists what changed for one file, the exact
deltas, and any new code that was added. Files that did NOT change are
listed at the end so it is clear nothing was missed.

=================================================================
 1. THE BIG CHANGE (summarized)
=================================================================
A bug-fix + UX round driven by the app's real usage:

  - "SAVE TO MEMORY" ON THE REAL CHAT UI. The user chats on the
    standalone `static/chat.html` page (the window.open target), NOT the
    index.html flyout widget the V1.8 header toggle was built for. So the
    standalone page gained its own "Save to memory" checkbox in the
    header, plus the `rag` flag on both message creation and finalize:

      * new header toggle `#sc-rag` (`.sc-rag-toggle`) rendered before
        the Save button, defaulting to `settings.rag.commitOnSave`;
      * `sendChat()` now POSTs `rag`, so a chat created from chat.html
        stamps the server session flag on the FIRST message too;
      * `finalizeActiveChat()` now POSTs `{ title, rag }` to
        `/api/chats/end` on every save.

  - "SAVED CHATS DIDN'T LAND WHERE I CONFIGURED". Root cause: paths are
    resolved ONCE at import in app/paths.py, and the running server was
    started BEFORE the user saved `dataDir: "E:\\data\\rag_store"` and
    `ragDbPath: "E:\\data\\db"`. It kept writing transcripts to the old
    `chatSavePath` (`E:\saved from app`) and the store to the old
    `data\rag_db`. Two fixes:

      * `app/paths.py` now snapshots the stored path keys at import and
        exposes `restart_needed()`; `GET /api/settings` returns
        `restartNeeded: true` when the file changed since the server
        started, and the configuration UI shows an amber warning banner
        telling the user to restart the server.
      * Field notes in the config form now spell out that transcripts
        follow "Chat save path" (NOT the Data folder; blank =
        `<dataDir>\chatlog\agent-text-records`) and that path changes
        apply after a restart; a new ragDbPath starts an empty store.

  - CONFIG DATA MIGRATED (filesystem, not code). To match the user's
    configured folders:
      * `chatSavePath` was REMOVED from `static/config/app_settings.json`
        so transcripts now resolve to `<dataDir>/chatlog/agent-text-records`
        (`E:\data\rag_store\chatlog\agent-text-records`).
      * `E:\saved from app\*.txt` (5 transcripts) moved to that new folder.
      * `data\chatlog\chatRecord.jsonl` + `.active-chat.json` moved to
        `E:\data\rag_store\chatlog\` so the saved-chat dropdown survives.
      * Empty `data\chat-transcripts` and `data\chatlog` removed. The old
        RAG store `data\rag_db` is LOCKED by the running server (chroma
        file handles); it stays until the server is stopped, then the
        user deletes it. The fresh store creates at `E:\data\db` on next
        start (auto-ingest is on, so it re-indexes the moved transcripts
        on first search).

  - DOCS RE-SYNC. `documentation/_make_snapshot.py` + this v1-9 delta;
    `APP_SNAPSHOT.md` regenerated (still 52 files, 9 changelogs);
    small README note added.

 3. FILE: app/paths.py                          (CHANGED - THIS ROUND)
=================================================================
  3.1  Module docstring note added ("`restart_needed()` tells callers
       when a restart is required.").
  3.2  Right after the import-time config load, a snapshot of the path
       keys is captured:
           _PATH_KEYS = ("dataDir", "chatSavePath", "ragDbPath")
           _path_keys_at_import = {key: _get_text(_cfg.get(key))
                                   for key in _PATH_KEYS}
  3.3  New public function:
           def restart_needed() -> bool:
               """True when stored path settings changed since import -
               the running server is still using the old resolved
               locations until a restart."""
               current = _load_app_settings()
               for key in _PATH_KEYS:
                   if (_get_text(current.get(key))
                           != _path_keys_at_import.get(key)):
                       return True
               return False
````
